1: import type { EngineeringStore, SessionRepository } from "@osstack/core";
2: import { EngineeringRpcRequests } from "@osstack/protocol";
3: import type { EngineeringAccess } from "./engineering-access.js";
4: import type { ExecutionLease } from "./governed-execution.js";
5: 
6: type Session = NonNullable<ReturnType<SessionRepository["getSession"]>>;
7: type Dispatch = ReturnType<EngineeringStore["commitDispatch"]>;
8: type Admission = { lease: ExecutionLease; controller: AbortController; release(): Promise<void> };
9: export interface EngineeringHostPorts {
10:   getSession(id: string): Session | undefined;
11:   qualified(session: Session): boolean;
12:   draining(): boolean;
13:   active(sessionId: string): boolean;
14:   acquire(session: Session, messageId: string): Promise<Admission>;
15:   execute(dispatch: Dispatch, session: Session, admission: Admission): Promise<void>;
16:   stop(runId: string): boolean;
17: }
18: 
19: /** No second sender/outbox. This host only drives first dispatch of the existing receipt. */
20: export class EngineeringHost {
21:   private readonly pending = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
22:   private closed = false;
23:   private timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined;
24:   private cursor: { created_at: number; action_id: string } | undefined;
25:   private missionCursor: { created_at: number; mission_id: string } | undefined;
26:   constructor(readonly store: EngineeringStore, readonly access: EngineeringAccess, private readonly ports: EngineeringHostPorts) {}
27: 
28:   async handle(method: string, params: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
29:     if (this.closed) throw new Error("engineering_unavailable");
30:     if (!(method in EngineeringRpcRequests)) throw new Error("unknown_method");
31:     const schema = EngineeringRpcRequests[method as keyof typeof EngineeringRpcRequests];
32:     const parsed = schema.safeParse(params);
33:     if (!parsed.success) throw new Error("bad_request");
34:     const { credential, ...input } = parsed.data;
35:     const actor = this.access.authenticate(credential);
36:     // Reparse under the concrete schema below: the wire union cannot infer the method correlation.
37:     if (method === "engineering.adopt") {
38:       if (this.ports.draining()) throw new Error("draining");
39:       const p = EngineeringRpcRequests["engineering.adopt"].parse(params);
40:       const session = this.ports.getSession(p.session_id);
41:       if (!session || !this.ports.qualified(session)) throw new Error("executor_not_qualified");
42:       this.access.assertOutsideRepository(session.repo_path);
43:       const { credential: _credential, ...request } = p;
44:       return this.store.adopt(actor, request);
45:     }
46:     if (!("mission_id" in input)) throw new Error("bad_request");
47:     const mission = this.store.getMission(input.mission_id);
48:     if (!mission) throw new Error("not_found");
49:     this.access.assertOutsideCanonicalRepository(mission.repo_path);
50:     switch (method) {
51:       case "engineering.observe": {
52:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
53:         return this.store.observe(actor, p);
54:       }
55:       case "engineering.decide": {
56:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
57:         // Read-only receipt replay is allowed while drained or at capacity. The
58:         // domain still verifies actor/scope and the complete immutable payload.
59:         const replay = this.store.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM engineering_requests WHERE principal_id=? AND client_session_id=? AND request_id=? AND operation='decide'").get(actor.principal_id, actor.client_session_id, p.action_id);
60:         if (!replay && this.ports.draining()) throw new Error("draining");
61:         if (!replay && this.pending.size >= 16) throw new Error("engineering_busy");
62:         const result = this.store.decide(actor, p);
63:         if (!result.replay) this.schedule(result.action_id);
64:         return result;
65:       }
66:       case "engineering.defer": {
67:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
68:         return this.store.defer(actor, p);
69:       }
70:       case "engineering.cancel": {
71:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
72:         const result = this.store.cancel(actor, p);
73:         if (!result.replay) {
74:           const requested = this.ports.stop(result.run_id);
75:           this.store.recordStopReceipt(result.mission.mission_id, result.stop_intent_id, "stop_unknown", {
76:             abort_requested: requested,
77:             reason: requested ? "abort is a request; containment exit is not proven" : "no live invocation in this daemon; prior worker exit is unknown",
78:           });
79:         }
80:         return result;
81:       }
82:       case "engineering.endObservation": {
83:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
84:         return this.store.endObservation(actor, p);
85:       }
86:       case "engineering.reconcile": {
87:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
88:         return this.store.reconcile(actor, p);
89:       }
90:       default: throw new Error("unknown_method");
91:     }
92:   }
93: 
94:   /** No model polling. Bounded pages let a denied old action coexist with later work. */
95:   start(): void {
96:     if (this.closed || this.timer) return;
97:     this.tick();
98:     this.timer = setInterval(() => this.tick(), 1000);
99:     this.timer.unref();
100:   }
101:   private tick(): void {
102:     if (this.closed || this.ports.draining()) return;
103:     this.recover();
104:     try {
105:       const rows = this.store.listActiveMissions(16, this.missionCursor);
106:       for (const row of rows) {
107:         try { this.store.refresh(row.mission_id); } catch { /* Retain original source obligations. */ }
108:       }
109:       const last = rows.at(-1);
110:       this.missionCursor = rows.length === 16 && last ? { created_at: last.created_at, mission_id: last.mission_id } : undefined;
111:     } catch { /* Fencing failures cannot become observer or dispatch authority. */ }
112:   }
113:   /** Only persisted reserved phases are eligible, never committed/legacy. */
114:   recover(): void {
115:     if (this.closed || this.ports.draining()) return;
116:     try {
117:       const rows = this.store.listReserved(16, this.cursor);
118:       for (const row of rows) this.schedule(row.action_id);
119:       const last = rows.at(-1);
120:       this.cursor = rows.length === 16 && last ? { created_at: last.created_at, action_id: last.action_id } : undefined;
121:     } catch { /* A stale owner or unavailable database cannot authorize dispatch. */ }
122:   }
123:   private schedule(actionId: string): void {
124:     if (this.closed || this.ports.draining() || this.pending.has(actionId) || this.pending.size >= 16) return;
125:     // Let the durable received response become observable before expensive admission.
126:     const pending = new Promise<void>(resolve => setImmediate(resolve))
127:       .then(() => this.dispatch(actionId))
128:       .catch(() => { /* The authoritative reserved/unknown receipt remains inspectable; never retry committed. */ })
129:       .finally(() => this.pending.delete(actionId));
130:     this.pending.set(actionId, pending);
131:   }
132:   private async dispatch(actionId: string): Promise<void> {
133:     if (this.closed || this.ports.draining()) return;
134:     const row = this.store.getReserved(actionId);
135:     if (!row) return;
136:     const session = this.ports.getSession(row.session_id);
137:     if (!session || !this.ports.qualified(session) || this.ports.active(session.session_id)) return;
138:     this.access.assertOutsideRepository(session.repo_path);
139:     const admission = await this.ports.acquire(session, row.message_id);
140:     let handedOff = false;
141:     try {
142:       if (this.closed || this.ports.draining() || admission.controller.signal.aborted) return;
143:       // The latest authority is read after asynchronous admission, immediately before synchronous CAS.
144:       const actor = this.access.refresh(row.owner_principal_id, row.client_session_id);
145:       this.access.assertOutsideRepository(session.repo_path);
146:       const dispatch = this.store.commitDispatch(actionId, actor);
147:       handedOff = true;
148:       // A pilot reply turn has a host-enforced five-minute wall-clock budget.
149:       // Abort requests cancellation; the existing helper retains admission if
150:       // actual adapter exit remains unproved.
151:       const deadline = setTimeout(() => admission.controller.abort(), 300_000);
152:       deadline.unref();
153:       try { await this.ports.execute(dispatch, session, admission); }
154:       finally { clearTimeout(deadline); }
155:     } finally { if (!handedOff) await admission.release(); }
156:   }
157:   async close(): Promise<void> {
158:     this.closed = true;
159:     if (this.timer) clearInterval(this.timer);
160:     this.timer = undefined;
161:     await Promise.allSettled(this.pending.values());
162:   }
163: }

===== FILE packages/cli/src/governed-execution.ts sha256=471d5ba2b17392ce4dce2b31df64673f71bac02b5b5a5ae58d7ef4e703c93f75 =====
1: import { join } from "node:path";
2: import { acquireRepoWriteLock, FifoSemaphore, SemaphoreAcquireAbortedError, type SemaphoreLease, type RepoWriteLock } from '@osstack/autonomy';
3: import { resolveRepositoryScope } from '@osstack/core';
4: 
5: export interface ExecutionLease {
6:   /** Idempotent after physical release, including retries of a lost DB acknowledgement. */
7:   release(): Promise<void>;
8:   /** Keep ownership until a trusted caller has confirmed external exit. */
9:   retain(reason: string): void;
10:   /** Trusted host only; never accept a worker-provided proof callback.
11:    * Idempotent after release: recovery must never release semaphore permits twice. */
12:   releaseAfterReconciliation?(verify: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void>;
13:   /** Assert this exact canonical writer lease is still live. */
14:   assertActive?(): Promise<void>;
15:   /** Scoped synchronous file operations under the canonical writer lock. This
16:    * capability is absent on read leases. Failures retain admission until recovery. */
17:   withWriteGuard?<T>(workspacePath: string, operation: (assertActive: () => void) => T): Promise<T>;
18: }
19: export interface AdmissionInput {
20:   kind: 'read' | 'write';
21:   runId: string;
22:   /** Supplied by the daemon registration, never by an adapter context. */
23:   workspacePath?: string;
24:   repoId?: string;
25:   signal?: AbortSignal;
26:   authorize?: () => Promise<void>;
27:   /** Host-only synchronous proof: acquisition rejected with no lease or cleanup obligation. */
28:   onNotAcquired?: () => void;
29: }
30: export class GovernedExecution {
31:   private readonly activeRuns = new Set<string>();
32:   private readonly quarantinedRepositories = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
33:   private quarantine(repoId: string, runId: string): void {
34:     const owners = this.quarantinedRepositories.get(repoId) ?? new Set<string>();
35:     owners.add(runId); this.quarantinedRepositories.set(repoId, owners);
36:   }
37:   constructor(private readonly options: {
38:     reads: FifoSemaphore;
39:     writes: FifoSemaphore;
40:     repositories: Map<string, FifoSemaphore>;
41:     checkHealth: () => Promise<void>;
42:   }) {}
43: 
44:   get hasWriters(): boolean { return this.options.writes.in_use > 0; }
45: 
46:   async acquire(input: AdmissionInput): Promise<ExecutionLease> {
47:     const cleanup = { confirmed: true };
48:     try { return await this.acquireOwned(input, cleanup); }
49:     catch (error) {
50:       if (cleanup.confirmed && !this.activeRuns.has(input.runId)) input.onNotAcquired?.();
51:       throw error;
52:     }
53:   }
54: 
55:   private async acquireOwned(input: AdmissionInput, cleanup: { confirmed: boolean }): Promise<ExecutionLease> {
56:     const check = async (): Promise<void> => {
57:       if (input.signal?.aborted) throw new SemaphoreAcquireAbortedError();
58:       if (input.kind === 'write') await this.options.checkHealth();
59:       await input.authorize?.();
60:     };
61:     await check();
62:     const scope = input.workspacePath === undefined ? undefined
63:       : await resolveRepositoryScope(input.workspacePath, input.repoId === undefined ? {} : { repoId: input.repoId });
64:     if (input.kind === 'write' && !scope) throw new Error('trusted repository scope required for writes');
65:     if (input.kind === "write" && scope && this.quarantinedRepositories.has(scope.repoId)) throw new Error("repository external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required");
66:     if (this.activeRuns.has(input.runId)) {
67:       cleanup.confirmed = false;
68:       throw new Error("run already admitted or queued");
69:     }
70:     this.activeRuns.add(input.runId);
71:     let local: SemaphoreLease | undefined;
72:     let global: SemaphoreLease | undefined;
73:     let lock: RepoWriteLock | undefined;
74:     let queue: FifoSemaphore | undefined;
75:     const releasePermits = (): void => {
76:       global?.release(); local?.release();
77:       this.activeRuns.delete(input.runId);
78:       if (scope && queue?.in_use === 0 && queue.pending === 0) this.options.repositories.delete(scope.repoId);
79:     };
80:     let released = false;
81:     let retained = false;
82:     let writeGuardActive = false;
83:     // Start as soon as the filesystem lock exists, including the final policy
84:     // wait. Once admitted, a renewal failure retains the unknown producer.
85:     let renewing: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
86:     let timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined;
87:     const startRenewal = () => {
88:       timer = setInterval(() => {
89:         if (writeGuardActive) return;
90:         renewing = renewing.then(async () => {
91:           if (!released && !writeGuardActive) await lock!.renew();
92:         }).catch((error: unknown) => {
93:           retained = true;
94:           if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
95:           process.stderr.write(`repository lease renewal failed; retained ${input.runId}: ${String(error)}\n`);
96:           if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
97:         });
98:       }, 20_000);
99:       timer.unref();
100:     };
101:     try {
102:       if (input.kind === 'write' && scope) {
103:         queue = this.options.repositories.get(scope.repoId) ?? new FifoSemaphore(1);
104:         this.options.repositories.set(scope.repoId, queue);
105:         local = await queue.acquire(input.signal);
106:       }
107:       global = await (input.kind === 'read' ? this.options.reads : this.options.writes).acquire(input.signal);
108:       if (input.kind === 'write' && scope) {
109:         // A failed filesystem acquisition may have left an ownership obligation.
110:         cleanup.confirmed = false;
111:         lock = await acquireRepoWriteLock({ root_dir: join(scope.commonDir, "osstack", "writer-locks"), recover_stale: false, repo_id: scope.repoId, autonomy_run_id: input.runId });
112:         // Authorization may wait behind another branch's synchronous work.
113:         // Start ownership renewal before that wait, not after publishing admission.
114:         startRenewal();
115:       }
116:       if (input.kind === "write" && scope && this.quarantinedRepositories.has(scope.repoId)) throw new Error("repository external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required");
117:       await check();
118:       await renewing;
119:       if (retained) throw new Error("repository lease renewal failed during admission");
120:       await lock?.assertActive();
121:       if (retained) throw new Error("repository lease renewal failed during admission");
122:       if (input.signal?.aborted) throw new SemaphoreAcquireAbortedError();
123:     } catch (error) {
124:       if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
125:       await renewing;
126:       try {
127:         await lock?.release();
128:         if (lock) cleanup.confirmed = true;
129:         // No executor was admitted. Remove only this rejected acquisition's quarantine.
130:         if (scope) {
131:           const owners = this.quarantinedRepositories.get(scope.repoId);
132:           owners?.delete(input.runId);
133:           if (owners?.size === 0) this.quarantinedRepositories.delete(scope.repoId);
134:         }
135:       } catch (releaseError) {
136:         if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
137:         throw new AggregateError([error, releaseError], 'admission rejected and ownership cleanup failed');
138:       } finally { releasePermits(); }
139:       throw error;
140:     }
141:     let reconciled: Promise<void> | undefined;
142:     let releasing: Promise<void> | undefined;
143:     return {
144:       ...(lock && scope ? { withWriteGuard: async <T>(workspacePath: string, operation: (assertActive: () => void) => T): Promise<T> => {
145:         if (workspacePath !== scope.workspacePath) throw new Error('write guard workspace mismatch');
146:         if (released || retained || writeGuardActive || reconciled || releasing) throw new Error('execution lease unavailable');
147:         writeGuardActive = true;
148:         try {
149:           await renewing;
150:           if (released || retained || releasing) throw new Error('execution lease unavailable');
151:           // Do not let the timer contend with our metadata guard. Renew on both
152:           // sides while still excluding release; renewal never revives expiry.
153:           await lock!.renew();
154:           const value = await lock!.withActiveGuard(check => {
155:             const assertActive = () => {
156:               if (released || retained || releasing) throw new Error('execution lease unavailable');
157:               check();
158:             };
159:             assertActive(); const result = operation(assertActive);
160:             if (result !== null && (typeof result === 'object' || typeof result === 'function') && 'then' in result) {
161:               void Promise.resolve(result).catch(() => {});
162:               throw new Error('write guard operation must be synchronous');
163:             }
164:             assertActive(); return result;
165:           });
166:           await lock!.renew();
167:           return value;
168:         } catch (error) {
169:           retained = true; if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
170:           this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
171:           throw error;
172:         } finally { writeGuardActive = false; }
173:       } } : {}),
174:       assertActive: async () => {
175:         if (released || retained || releasing) throw new Error("execution lease unavailable");
176:         await lock?.assertActive();
177:         if (released || retained || releasing) throw new Error("execution lease unavailable");
178:       },
179:       releaseAfterReconciliation: (verify) => {
180:         if (typeof verify !== 'function') return Promise.reject(new Error('trusted reconciliation verifier required'));
181:         if (releasing || released || reconciled) {
182:           const pending = releasing ?? reconciled;
183:           return (async () => {
184:             const result: unknown = await verify();
185:             if (result !== undefined) throw new Error('invalid reconciliation authority');
186:             await pending;
187:           })();
188:         }
189:         if (writeGuardActive) return Promise.reject(new Error('write guard in progress'));
190:         reconciled = (async () => {
191:           // Stop renewal first. No authority is freed until the verifier succeeds
192:           // and the canonical token is released under its filesystem guard.
193:           if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
194:           await renewing;
195:           const result: unknown = await verify();
196:           if (result !== undefined) throw new Error('invalid reconciliation authority');
197:           await lock?.release();
198:           released = true; retained = false;
199:           if (scope) {
200:             const owners = this.quarantinedRepositories.get(scope.repoId);
201:             owners?.delete(input.runId);
202:             if (owners?.size === 0) this.quarantinedRepositories.delete(scope.repoId);
203:           }
204:           releasePermits();
205:         })().catch((error: unknown) => {
206:           retained = true;
207:           if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
208:           throw error;
209:         }).finally(() => { reconciled = undefined; });
210:         return reconciled;
211:       },
212:       retain: (reason) => { retained = true; if (timer) clearInterval(timer); if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId); process.stderr.write(`osstack: retaining write lease ${input.runId}: ${reason}\n`); },
213:       release: () => {
214:         if (releasing) return releasing;
215:         if (reconciled) return reconciled;
216:         if (writeGuardActive) return Promise.reject(new Error('write guard in progress'));
217:         if (released) return Promise.resolve();
218:         if (retained) return Promise.reject(new Error("external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required before release"));
219:         releasing = (async () => {
220:           if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
221:           await renewing;
222:           if (retained) throw new Error("external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required before release");
223:           // Physical completion, not starting its asynchronous release, is the
224:           // final state. Concurrent release/reconciliation callers share this wait.
225:           try { await lock?.release(); }
226:           catch (error) {
227:             retained = true;
228:             if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
229:             process.stderr.write(`repository lock release failed; retained ${input.runId}: ${String(error)}\n`);
230:             throw error;
231:           }
232:           released = true;
233:           releasePermits();
234:         })().finally(() => { releasing = undefined; });
235:         return releasing;
236:       },
237:     };
238:   }
239: }

307:         next_action:

309:       };
310:     });
311:   }
312:   recover(): void {
313:     const rows = this.store.db
314:       .prepare(
315:         "SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE state IN ('reserved','uncertain')"
316:       )
317:       .all() as Action[];
318:     for (const row of rows) this.reconcile(row);
319:   }
320:   reconcile(action: Action, now = Date.now()): Action {
321:     return this.store.write(this.owner, () => {
322:       const phase = getEngineeringDispatchPhase(this.store.db, action.message_id);
323:       if (phase?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch") {
324:         if (!isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(this.store.db, action.message_id)) return { ...action, state: "needs_reconciliation" };
325:         return { ...action, state: "cancelled_before_dispatch" };
326:       }
327:       if (
328:         this.store.db
329:           .prepare(
330:             "SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?"
331:           )
332:           .get(action.message_id)
333:       )
334:         return { ...action, state: "abandoned_unknown" };
335:       const receipt = this.store.db
336:         .prepare("SELECT state FROM session_messages WHERE message_id=?")
337:         .get(action.message_id) as { state: string } | undefined;
338:       if (phase?.phase === "reserved") {
339:         return { ...action, state: action.state === "reserved" && receipt?.state === "accepted" ? "reserved" : "needs_reconciliation" };
340:       }
341:       const state =
342:         receipt?.state === "applied"
343:           ? "applied"
344:           : receipt?.state === "failed"
345:           ? "uncertain"
346:           : "uncertain";
347:       this.store.db
348:         .prepare(
349:           "UPDATE attention_actions SET state=?,updated_at=? WHERE action_id=?"
350:         )
351:         .run(state, now, action.action_id);
352:       this.store.db
353:         .prepare(
354:           "UPDATE attention_items SET state=?,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id=? AND state<>?"
355:         )
356:         .run(
357:           state === "applied" ? "answer_applied" : "answer_uncertain",
358:           now,
359:           action.item_id,
360:           state === "applied" ? "answer_applied" : "answer_uncertain"
361:         );
362:       return { ...action, state, updated_at: now };
363:     });
364:   }

45:         JOIN runs r ON r.run_id=s.run_id AND r.task_id=s.task_id AND r.session_id=s.session_id AND r.agent_id=s.agent_id AND r.tenant_id IS NULL
46:         WHERE s.session_id=? AND s.task_id=?`).get(subject.subject_id, scope.task_id) as { session_id: string; run_id: string; request_sha256: string; agent_id: string; repo_path: string; version: number; state: string } | undefined;
47:       if (!session || !AgentSessionState.safeParse(session.state).success || session.version !== subject.source_version || attentionDigest([session.session_id, session.run_id, session.request_sha256, session.agent_id, session.repo_path]) !== subject.binding_hash) return base;
48:       try { if (realpathSync(session.repo_path) !== scope.repo_path) return base; } catch { return base; }
49:       const page = <T>(sql: string, countSql: string, params: string[]): EvidencePage<T> => {
50:         const found = store.db.prepare(sql).all(...params) as T[];
51:         if (found.some(row => Object.values(row as Record<string, unknown>).some(value => typeof value === "string" && value.length > 4096))) throw Error("oversized_evidence_identity");
52:         const count = (store.db.prepare(countSql).get(...params) as { n: number }).n;
53:         return { total: count, truncated: count > 20, rows: found.slice(0,20) };
54:       };
55:       const messages = page<MessageEvidence>(`SELECT substr(m.message_id,1,4097) message_id,substr(m.turn_id,1,4097) turn_id,m.state,r.disposition FROM session_messages m LEFT JOIN attention_receipt_resolutions r
56:         ON r.message_id=m.message_id AND r.session_id=m.session_id WHERE m.session_id=? AND m.direction='inbound' ORDER BY m.created_at,m.message_id LIMIT 20`,
57:         "SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? AND direction='inbound'", [session.session_id]);
58:       for (const message of messages.rows) {
59:         const phase = getEngineeringDispatchPhase(store.db, message.message_id);
60:         if (phase) message.dispatch_phase = phase.phase;
61:         if (phase?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch") message.disposition = isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(store.db, message.message_id) ? "cancelled_before_dispatch" : "needs_reconciliation";
62:       }
63:       const actions = page<ActionEvidence>(`SELECT substr(a.action_id,1,4097) action_id,substr(a.item_id,1,4097) item_id,substr(a.message_id,1,4097) message_id,substr(a.state,1,65) state FROM attention_actions a JOIN attention_items i ON i.item_id=a.item_id AND i.scope_id=a.scope_id
64:         JOIN session_messages m ON m.message_id=a.message_id AND m.session_id=i.session_id AND m.turn_id=i.turn_id WHERE a.scope_id=? AND i.session_id=? ORDER BY a.created_at,a.action_id LIMIT 20`,
65:         `SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM attention_actions a JOIN attention_items i ON i.item_id=a.item_id AND i.scope_id=a.scope_id
66:         JOIN session_messages m ON m.message_id=a.message_id AND m.session_id=i.session_id AND m.turn_id=i.turn_id WHERE a.scope_id=? AND i.session_id=?`, [scope.scope_id, session.session_id]);
67:       const grants = page<GrantEvidence>(`SELECT substr(a.attempt_id,1,4097) attempt_id,substr(a.grant_id,1,4097) grant_id,a.status,substr(a.message_id,1,4097) message_id FROM session_continuation_attempts a JOIN session_continuation_grants g ON g.grant_id=a.grant_id WHERE g.session_id=? ORDER BY a.created_at,a.attempt_id LIMIT 20`,
68:         "SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM session_continuation_attempts a JOIN session_continuation_grants g ON g.grant_id=a.grant_id WHERE g.session_id=?", [session.session_id]);
69:       const unresolved = (store.db.prepare("SELECT message_id FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? AND direction='inbound' AND state IN ('pending','accepted','failed')").all(session.session_id) as Array<{ message_id: string }>).some(message => !isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(store.db, message.message_id));
70:       const activeGrant = store.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM session_continuation_attempts a JOIN session_continuation_grants g ON g.grant_id=a.grant_id WHERE g.session_id=? AND a.status IN ('checking','reserved') LIMIT 1").get(session.session_id);
71:       const missing = [...(unresolved ? ["terminal_receipt"] : []), ...(activeGrant ? ["continuation_receipt"] : [])];
72:       return { ...base, state: missing.length ? "unknown" : session.state === "awaiting_decision" ? "pending_human" : "observed", missing_evidence: missing,
73:         next_step: missing.length ? "inspect_existing_receipts" : session.state === "awaiting_decision" ? "answer_current_decision" : "inspect_source", messages, actions, grant_attempts: grants };
74:       };
75:       try { return readSubject(); } catch { return { ...base, missing_evidence: ["invalid_evidence"] }; }
76:     });
77:     return { scope_id: scope.scope_id, canAutoRetry: false as const, process_liveness: "unknown" as const, total, returned: subjects.length, truncated: rows.length > limit,
78:       next_after: rows.length > limit ? String(rows[limit - 1]!.cursor) : null, subjects };
79:   }).deferred();
80: }

1: import { createHash, randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
2: import { realpathSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
3: import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
4: import { SessionDecision } from "@osstack/protocol";
5: import { withWrite } from "./db.js";

7: export function attentionDigest(value: unknown): string {
8:   const canonical = (v: unknown): string => {
9:     if (Array.isArray(v)) return `[${v.map(canonical).join(",")}]`;
10:     if (v !== null && typeof v === "object")
11:       return `{${Object.entries(v)
12:         .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0))
13:         .map(([k, x]) => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonical(x)}`)
14:         .join(",")}}`;
15:     return JSON.stringify(v);
16:   };
17:   return createHash("sha256").update(canonical(value)).digest("hex");
18: }

20:   instance_id: string;
21:   generation: number;
22:   home_id: string;
23: }
24: export interface AttentionScope {
25:   scope_id: string;

55:     decision: SessionDecision.parse(JSON.parse(decision_json)),
56:   };

58: export class AttentionStore {
59:   constructor(readonly db: Database.Database, readonly path: string) {}
60:   /** Only called AFTER the daemon's process lock is acquired. Not an executor lease. */
61:   claimOwner(instanceId: string): AttentionOwner {
62:     return withWrite(this.db, this.path, () => {
63:       this.db
64:         .prepare(
65:           `INSERT INTO daemon_owner(singleton,home_id,instance_id,generation) VALUES(1,?,?,1)
66:         ON CONFLICT(singleton) DO UPDATE SET instance_id=excluded.instance_id,generation=generation+1`
67:         )
68:         .run(randomUUID(), instanceId);
69:       return this.db
70:         .prepare(
71:           "SELECT home_id,instance_id,generation FROM daemon_owner WHERE singleton=1"
72:         )
73:         .get() as AttentionOwner;
74:     });
75:   }
76:   assertOwner(owner: AttentionOwner): void {
77:     if (
78:       !this.db
79:         .prepare(
80:           "SELECT 1 FROM daemon_owner WHERE singleton=1 AND home_id=? AND instance_id=? AND generation=?"
81:         )
82:         .get(owner.home_id, owner.instance_id, owner.generation)
83:     )
84:       throw new Error("stale_owner");
85:   }
86:   write<T>(owner: AttentionOwner, fn: () => T): T {
87:     if (this.db.inTransaction) {
88:       this.assertOwner(owner);
89:       return fn();
90:     }
91:     return withWrite(this.db, this.path, () => {
92:       this.assertOwner(owner);
93:       return fn();
94:     });
95:   }
96:   registerScope(
97:     owner: AttentionOwner,
98:     taskId: string,
99:     repoPath: string
100:   ): AttentionScope {
101:     const canonicalRepo = realpathSync(repoPath);
102:     if (!statSync(canonicalRepo).isDirectory())
103:       throw new Error("repository_directory_required");
104:     return this.write(owner, () => {
105:       const task = this.db
106:         .prepare(
107:           "SELECT created_at FROM tasks WHERE task_id=? AND tenant_id IS NULL"
108:         )
109:         .get(taskId) as { created_at: number } | undefined;
110:       if (!task) throw new Error("not_found");
111:       if (
112:         (
113:           this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM attention_scopes").get() as {
114:             n: number;
115:           }
116:         ).n >= 100 &&
117:         !this.db
118:           .prepare(
119:             "SELECT 1 FROM attention_scopes WHERE task_id=? AND repo_path=?"
120:           )
121:           .get(taskId, canonicalRepo)
122:       )
123:         throw new Error("scope_capacity");
124:       const scopeId = attentionDigest([
125:         owner.home_id,
126:         taskId,
127:         canonicalRepo,
128:         task.created_at,
129:       ]);
130:       this.db
131:         .prepare(
132:           `INSERT INTO attention_scopes(scope_id,task_id,repo_path,generation) VALUES(?,?,?,?)
133:         ON CONFLICT(scope_id) DO NOTHING`
134:         )
135:         .run(scopeId, taskId, canonicalRepo, owner.generation);
136:       return this.getScope(scopeId)!;
137:     });
138:   }
139:   getScope(scopeId: string): AttentionScope | undefined {
140:     return this.db
141:       .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_scopes WHERE scope_id=?")
142:       .get(scopeId) as AttentionScope | undefined;
143:   }
144:   listScopes(): AttentionScope[] {
145:     return this.db
146:       .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_scopes ORDER BY scope_id")
147:       .all() as AttentionScope[];
148:   }
149:   getItem(scopeId: string, itemId: string): AttentionItem | undefined {
150:     const row = this.db
151:       .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_items WHERE scope_id=? AND item_id=?")
152:       .get(scopeId, itemId) as ItemRow | undefined;
153:     return row ? parseItem(row) : undefined;
154:   }
155:   listItems(scopeId: string, after = "", limit = 100): AttentionItem[] {
156:     if (!Number.isSafeInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 100)
157:       throw new Error("invalid_limit");
158:     return (
159:       this.db
160:         .prepare(
161:           "SELECT * FROM attention_items WHERE scope_id=? AND item_id>? ORDER BY item_id LIMIT ?"
162:         )
163:         .all(scopeId, after, limit) as ItemRow[]
164:     ).map(parseItem);
165:   }
166:   projectDecision(
167:     owner: AttentionOwner,
168:     input: {
169:       scopeId: string;
170:       sessionId: string;
171:       turnId: string;
172:       sourceVersion: number;
173:       decision: unknown;
174:       now: number;
175:     }
176:   ): AttentionItem {
177:     const decision = SessionDecision.parse(input.decision);
178:     const contentHash = attentionDigest(decision);
179:     const itemId = attentionDigest([
180:       input.scopeId,
181:       input.sessionId,
182:       input.turnId,
183:       decision.decision_id,
184:     ]);
185:     if (
186:       !Number.isSafeInteger(input.sourceVersion) ||
187:       input.sourceVersion < 1 ||
188:       !Number.isFinite(input.now)
189:     )
190:       throw new Error("invalid_source_version");
191:     return this.write(owner, () => {
192:       if (!this.getScope(input.scopeId)) throw new Error("not_found");
193:       const existing = this.getItem(input.scopeId, itemId);
194:       if (existing && existing.content_hash !== contentHash)
195:         throw new Error("decision_content_conflict");
196:       if (existing && existing.source_version > input.sourceVersion)
197:         throw new Error("stale_source");
198:       this.db
199:         .prepare(
200:           `INSERT INTO attention_items(item_id,scope_id,session_id,turn_id,decision_id,content_hash,decision_json,source_version,created_at,updated_at)
201:         VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(item_id) DO UPDATE SET
202:         revision=revision+CASE WHEN source_version<>excluded.source_version OR state='source_missing' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
203:         state=CASE WHEN state='source_missing' THEN 'open' ELSE state END,
204:         source_version=excluded.source_version,updated_at=excluded.updated_at`
205:         )
206:         .run(
207:           itemId,
208:           input.scopeId,
209:           input.sessionId,
210:           input.turnId,
211:           decision.decision_id,
212:           contentHash,
213:           JSON.stringify(decision),
214:           input.sourceVersion,
215:           input.now,
216:           input.now
217:         );
218:       return this.getItem(input.scopeId, itemId)!;
219:     });
220:   }
221:   release(
222:     owner: AttentionOwner,
223:     scopeId: string,
224:     kind: string,
225:     subjectId: string,
226:     expectedVersion: number
227:   ): void {
228:     if (!["session", "run", "loop"].includes(kind))
229:       throw new Error("invalid_subject_kind");
230:     this.write(owner, () => {
231:       const row = this.db
232:         .prepare(
233:           "SELECT payload_json,source_version FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND kind=? AND subject_id=?"
234:         )
235:         .get(scopeId, kind, subjectId) as
236:         | { payload_json: string; source_version: number }
237:         | undefined;
238:       if (!row || row.source_version !== expectedVersion)
239:         throw new Error("subject_version_conflict");
240:       this.db
241:         .prepare(
242:           "UPDATE attention_subjects SET admitted=0,payload_json=? WHERE scope_id=? AND kind=? AND subject_id=?"
243:         )
244:         .run(
245:           JSON.stringify({
246:             ...JSON.parse(row.payload_json),
247:             reason: "owner_released",
248:           }),
249:           scopeId,
250:           kind,
251:           subjectId
252:         );
253:     });
254:   }
255:   markMissing(owner: AttentionOwner, scopeId: string, sessionId: string): void {
256:     this.write(owner, () =>
257:       this.db
258:         .prepare(
259:           `UPDATE attention_items SET state='source_missing',revision=revision+1
260:       WHERE scope_id=? AND session_id=? AND state='open'`
261:         )
262:         .run(scopeId, sessionId)
263:     );
264:   }
265: }

63:      OR (phase='dispatch_committed' AND committed_by_generation>0 AND stop_intent_id IS NULL AND proof_json IS NULL)

75: BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'dispatch_phase_immutable'); END;

1: import type Database from "better-sqlite3";

3: export interface EngineeringDispatchPhase {
4:   action_id: string; message_id: string; mission_id: string; principal_id: string; client_session_id: string;
5:   protocol_version: 1; phase: "reserved" | "dispatch_committed" | "cancelled_before_dispatch";
6:   source_instance_id: string; source_generation: number; original_turn_id: string;
7:   source_version: number; reservation_version: number; reserved_by_generation: number;
8:   committed_by_generation: number | null; stop_intent_id: string | null; proof_json: string | null;
9:   created_at: number; updated_at: number;
10: }
11: export function getEngineeringDispatchPhase(db: Database.Database, messageId: string): EngineeringDispatchPhase | undefined {
12:   return db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE message_id=?").get(messageId) as EngineeringDispatchPhase | undefined;
13: }
14: 
15: /** A phase label alone cannot release the uncertain-message guard. The original
16:  * message/action, immutable cancellation transition, and durable intent agree. */
17: export function isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(db: Database.Database, messageId: string): boolean {
18:   const phase = getEngineeringDispatchPhase(db, messageId);
19:   if (!phase || phase.phase !== "cancelled_before_dispatch") return false;
20:   const bound = db.prepare(`SELECT m.state,a.state action_state,a.action_id,a.message_id,i.session_id,i.turn_id,e.kind,e.payload_json
21:     FROM attention_actions a JOIN session_messages m ON m.message_id=a.message_id
22:     JOIN attention_items i ON i.item_id=a.item_id AND i.scope_id=a.scope_id
23:     JOIN engineering_missions x ON x.mission_id=? AND x.session_id=m.session_id AND x.scope_id=a.scope_id
24:     JOIN engineering_events e ON e.mission_id=x.mission_id
25:     WHERE a.action_id=? AND a.message_id=? AND i.session_id=m.session_id AND i.turn_id=m.turn_id AND e.seq=?`).get(
26:       phase.mission_id, phase.action_id, messageId, readProofSequence(phase.proof_json),
27:     ) as { state: string; action_state: string; action_id: string; message_id: string; session_id: string; turn_id: string; kind: string; payload_json: string } | undefined;
28:   if (!bound || bound.state === "applied" || bound.action_state !== "cancelled_before_dispatch" || bound.turn_id !== phase.original_turn_id || !["stop_requested", "observation_ended"].includes(bound.kind)
29:     || db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?").get(messageId)) return false;
30:   try {
31:     const proof = JSON.parse(phase.proof_json ?? "null") as Record<string, unknown> | null;
32:     const event = JSON.parse(bound.payload_json) as Record<string, unknown>;
33:     return proof?.["version"] === 1 && proof["prior_phase"] === "reserved"
34:       && proof["action_id"] === phase.action_id && proof["message_id"] === messageId
35:       && proof["stop_intent_id"] === phase.stop_intent_id && event["stop_intent_id"] === phase.stop_intent_id
36:       && event["session_id"] === bound.session_id && Array.isArray(event["action_ids"]) && event["action_ids"].includes(phase.action_id)
37:       && event["owner_generation"] === proof["owner_generation"] && Number.isSafeInteger(proof["owner_generation"]) && Number(proof["owner_generation"]) > 0;
38:   } catch { return false; }
39: }
40: function readProofSequence(value: string | null): number {
41:   try { const v = JSON.parse(value ?? "null")?.event_seq; return Number.isSafeInteger(v) ? v : -1; } catch { return -1; }
42: }
