62: /** Synchronous domain operations execute under the daemon's existing owner
63:  * fence. No process, network, Adapter, or model calls occur in this store. */
64: export class EngineeringStore {
65:   private readonly permits = new WeakSet<EngineeringDispatchPermit>();
66:   constructor(readonly attention: AttentionStore, readonly owner: AttentionOwner) {}
67:   get db(): Database.Database { return this.attention.db; }
68:   getMission(missionId: string): EngineeringMission {
69:     const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_missions WHERE mission_id=?").get(missionId) as EngineeringMission | undefined;
70:     if (!row) throw Error("not_found"); return row;
71:   }
72:   getDispatch(messageId: string) { return getEngineeringDispatchPhase(this.db, messageId); }
73:   listReserved(limit = 100, after?: { created_at: number; action_id: string }) {
74:     if (!Number.isSafeInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 100) throw Error("invalid_limit");
75:     if (after && (!Number.isSafeInteger(after.created_at) || after.created_at < 0 || !after.action_id || after.action_id.length > 256)) throw Error("invalid_cursor");
76:     return this.db.prepare(`SELECT p.action_id,p.mission_id,m.owner_principal_id,p.client_session_id,m.session_id,p.message_id,p.created_at
77:       FROM attention_dispatch_phases p JOIN engineering_missions m ON m.mission_id=p.mission_id
78:       WHERE p.phase='reserved' AND m.stop_intent_id IS NULL AND m.state NOT IN ('completed','failed','cancel_requested','observation_ended')
79:       AND (p.created_at>? OR (p.created_at=? AND p.action_id>?))
80:       ORDER BY p.created_at,p.action_id LIMIT ?`).all(after?.created_at ?? -1, after?.created_at ?? -1, after?.action_id ?? "", limit) as Array<{ action_id: string; mission_id: string; owner_principal_id: string; client_session_id: string; session_id: string; message_id: string; created_at: number }>;
81:   }
82:   getReserved(actionId: string) {
83:     return this.db.prepare(`SELECT p.action_id,p.mission_id,m.owner_principal_id,p.client_session_id,m.session_id,p.message_id,p.created_at
84:       FROM attention_dispatch_phases p JOIN engineering_missions m ON m.mission_id=p.mission_id
85:       WHERE p.action_id=? AND p.phase='reserved' AND m.stop_intent_id IS NULL AND m.state NOT IN ('completed','failed','cancel_requested','observation_ended')`).get(actionId) as ReturnType<EngineeringStore["listReserved"]>[number] | undefined;
86:   }
87:   listActiveMissions(limit = 16, after?: { created_at: number; mission_id: string }) {
88:     if (!Number.isSafeInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 100 || (after && (!Number.isSafeInteger(after.created_at) || after.created_at < 0 || !after.mission_id))) throw Error("invalid_limit");
89:     return this.db.prepare(`SELECT mission_id,created_at FROM engineering_missions WHERE state NOT IN ('completed','failed','cancel_requested','observation_ended')
90:       AND (created_at>? OR (created_at=? AND mission_id>?)) ORDER BY created_at,mission_id LIMIT ?`).all(after?.created_at ?? -1, after?.created_at ?? -1, after?.mission_id ?? "", limit) as Array<{ mission_id: string; created_at: number }>;
91:   }
92:   private write<T>(fn: () => T): T {
93:     if (this.db.pragma("synchronous", { simple: true }) !== 2) throw Error("durability_required");
94:     return this.attention.write(this.owner, fn);
95:   }
96:   private access(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, scope: string, right: "read" | "write" | "stop" | "operator"): void {
97:     if (!principal.principal_id || !principal.client_session_id || !principal[`${right}_scopes`].includes(scope)) throw Error("permission_denied");
98:   }
99:   private readable(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, missionId: string): EngineeringMission {
100:     const mission = this.getMission(missionId); this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "read"); return mission;
101:   }
102:   private steer(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, mission: EngineeringMission): void {
103:     this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "read"); this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "write");
104:     if (mission.owner_principal_id !== principal.principal_id) throw Error("permission_denied");
105:   }
106:   private controlBudget(missionId: string): void {
107:     if ((this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_requests WHERE mission_id=? AND operation NOT IN ('cancel','endObservation')").get(missionId) as { n: number }).n >= 256) throw Error("budget_exhausted");
108:   }
109:   private cas(mission: EngineeringMission, revision: number): void {
110:     if (mission.revision !== revision) throw Error("version_conflict");
111:     if (terminal.has(mission.state)) throw Error("mission_terminal");
112:   }
113:   private clock(now: number): void { if (!Number.isSafeInteger(now) || now < 0) throw Error("invalid_clock"); }
114:   private event(missionId: string, kind: string, actor: string | null, payload: unknown, now: number): number {
115:     return Number(this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_events(mission_id,kind,actor,payload_json,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)").run(missionId, kind, actor, JSON.stringify(payload), now).lastInsertRowid);
116:   }
117:   private bump(missionId: string, state: EngineeringMission["state"], now: number): EngineeringMission {
118:     this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_missions SET state=?,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE mission_id=?").run(state, now, missionId);
119:     return this.getMission(missionId);
120:   }
121:   private request(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, requestId: string, operation: string, payload: unknown): StoredRequest | undefined {
122:     const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_requests WHERE principal_id=? AND client_session_id=? AND request_id=?").get(principal.principal_id, principal.client_session_id, requestId) as StoredRequest | undefined;
123:     if (row && (row.operation !== operation || row.payload_hash !== attentionDigest(payload))) throw Error("action_conflict");
124:     return row;
125:   }
126:   private saveRequest(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, requestId: string, operation: string, payload: unknown, missionId: string, result: unknown, now: number): void {
127:     this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_requests VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(principal.principal_id, principal.client_session_id, requestId, operation, attentionDigest(payload), missionId, JSON.stringify(result), now);
128:   }
129:   private source(mission: Pick<EngineeringMission, "scope_id" | "task_id" | "run_id" | "session_id" | "source_instance_id" | "repo_path">) {
130:     const sessions = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path); const session = sessions.getSession(mission.session_id);
131:     const scope = this.attention.getScope(mission.scope_id);
132:     if (!session || !scope || session.task_id !== mission.task_id || session.run_id !== mission.run_id || scope.task_id !== mission.task_id
133:       || !session.native_session_id || engineeringSourceIdentity(session.session_id, session.native_session_id) !== mission.source_instance_id
134:       || realpathSync(session.repo_path) !== mission.repo_path || scope.repo_path !== mission.repo_path) throw Error("source_changed");
135:     if (!this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM tasks t JOIN runs r ON r.task_id=t.task_id WHERE t.task_id=? AND r.run_id=? AND r.session_id=? AND r.agent_id=? AND t.tenant_id IS NULL AND r.tenant_id IS NULL").get(mission.task_id, mission.run_id, mission.session_id, session.agent_id)) throw Error("executor_not_qualified");
136:     return { session, snapshot: sessions.latestSnapshot(mission.session_id) };
137:   }
138:   private ordinaryDecision(value: unknown): SessionDecision {
139:     const decision = SessionDecision.parse(value);
140:     const gate = decision.continuation?.gate_kind;
141:     if (gate === "risk_approval" || gate === "task_authorization") throw Error("signed_approval_required");
142:     if (gate === "phase_continue") throw Error("executor_not_qualified");
143:     return decision;
144:   }
145:   private ref(item: AttentionItem): EngineeringDecisionRef {
146:     return { kind: "session_decision", scope_id: item.scope_id, item_id: item.item_id, item_revision: item.revision, session_id: item.session_id,
147:       turn_id: item.turn_id, decision_id: item.decision_id, source_version: item.source_version, content_hash: item.content_hash };
148:   }
149:   private addWait(mission: EngineeringMission, snapshot: SessionSnapshot, now: number): void {
150:     if (!snapshot.turn_id) throw Error("source_unavailable");
151:     const decision = this.ordinaryDecision(snapshot.decision);
152:     const itemId = attentionDigest([mission.scope_id, mission.session_id, snapshot.turn_id, decision.decision_id]);
153:     const existing = this.db.prepare("SELECT mission_id FROM engineering_waits WHERE item_id=?").get(itemId) as { mission_id: string } | undefined;
154:     if (existing) {
155:       if (existing.mission_id !== mission.mission_id) throw Error("authority_already_adopted");
156:       return;
157:     }
158:     const item = this.attention.projectDecision(this.owner, { scopeId: mission.scope_id, sessionId: mission.session_id, turnId: snapshot.turn_id, sourceVersion: snapshot.version, decision, now });
159:     const prior = this.db.prepare("SELECT mission_id FROM engineering_waits WHERE item_id=?").get(item.item_id) as { mission_id: string } | undefined;
160:     if (prior && prior.mission_id !== mission.mission_id) throw Error("authority_already_adopted");
161:     if (prior) return;
162:     if ((this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_waits WHERE mission_id=?").get(mission.mission_id) as { n: number }).n >= 64) throw Error("budget_exhausted");
163:     const waitId = randomUUID();
164:     this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_waits VALUES(?,?,?,1,?,'open',NULL,NULL,NULL,0,?,?)").run(waitId, mission.mission_id, item.item_id, JSON.stringify(this.ref(item)), now, now);
165:     this.event(mission.mission_id, "decision_observed", null, { wait_id: waitId, decision_ref: this.ref(item) }, now);
166:   }
167:   private waits(missionId: string): WaitRow[] { return this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_waits WHERE mission_id=? ORDER BY created_at,wait_id LIMIT 100").all(missionId) as WaitRow[]; }
168:   private wait(missionId: string, waitId: string): WaitRow {
169:     const wait = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_waits WHERE mission_id=? AND wait_id=?").get(missionId, waitId) as WaitRow | undefined;
170:     if (!wait) throw Error("not_found"); return wait;
171:   }
172:   private setWait(wait: WaitRow, state: WaitRow["state"], now: number): void {
173:     if (wait.state !== state) this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET state=?,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE wait_id=?").run(state, now, wait.wait_id);
174:   }
175:   adopt(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringAdoptRequest, now = Date.now()) {
176:     const input = EngineeringAdoptRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now);
177:     this.access(principal, input.scope_id, "read");
178:     return this.write(() => {
179:       const prior = this.request(principal, input.request_id, "adopt", input);
180:       if (prior) return { mission: this.readable(principal, prior.mission_id), replay: true };
181:       this.access(principal, input.scope_id, "write");
182:       const sessions = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path); const session = sessions.getSession(input.session_id);
183:       if (!session || !session.resume_supported || !session.native_session_id) throw Error("executor_not_qualified");
184:       const scope = this.attention.getScope(input.scope_id);
185:       if (!scope || scope.task_id !== input.task_id) throw Error("scope_mismatch");
186:       const mission: EngineeringMission = { mission_id: randomUUID(), scope_id: input.scope_id, task_id: input.task_id, run_id: input.run_id, session_id: input.session_id,
187:         repo_path: scope.repo_path, source_instance_id: engineeringSourceIdentity(session.session_id, session.native_session_id), source_generation: 1,
188:         owner_principal_id: principal.principal_id, owner_client_session_id: principal.client_session_id, goal: input.goal, completion_level: "observed_turn", state: "awaiting_decision", revision: 1,
189:         stop_intent_id: null, created_at: now, updated_at: now, observed_at: now, policy_digest: POLICY_DIGEST, observation_proof_json: null };
190:       const { snapshot } = this.source(mission);
191:       if (snapshot.state !== "awaiting_decision") throw Error("session_not_awaiting_decision");
192:       this.ordinaryDecision(snapshot.decision);
193:       if (this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM engineering_missions WHERE session_id=? AND state NOT IN ('completed','failed','cancel_requested','observation_ended')").get(input.session_id)) throw Error("source_already_adopted");
194:       if ((this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_missions").get() as { n: number }).n >= 1000) throw Error("budget_exhausted");
195:       this.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO engineering_missions VALUES(@mission_id,@scope_id,@task_id,@run_id,@session_id,@repo_path,@source_instance_id,@source_generation,@owner_principal_id,@owner_client_session_id,@goal,@completion_level,@state,@revision,@stop_intent_id,@created_at,@updated_at,@observed_at,@policy_digest,@observation_proof_json)`).run(mission);
196:       this.addWait(mission, snapshot, now); this.event(mission.mission_id, "session_adopted", principal.principal_id, { session_id: mission.session_id, source_instance_id: mission.source_instance_id, completion_level: "observed_turn" }, now);
197:       this.saveRequest(principal, input.request_id, "adopt", input, mission.mission_id, {}, now);
198:       return { mission, replay: false };
199:     });
200:   }
201:   observe(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringObserveRequest, now = Date.now()) {
202:     const input = EngineeringObserveRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now);
203:     const readable = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
204:     return this.attention.write(this.owner, () => {
205:       this.refresh(readable.mission_id, now);
206:       const mission = this.getMission(input.mission_id);
207:       let snapshot: SessionSnapshot | null = null;
208:       try { snapshot = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).latestSnapshot(mission.session_id); } catch { /* Preserve observation and stop access after source removal. */ }
209:       const waits = this.waits(mission.mission_id).map((wait): EngineeringWait => {
210:         const { decision_ref_json, ...rest } = wait;
211:         const decision_ref = JSON.parse(decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
212:         const item = this.attention.getItem(mission.scope_id, wait.item_id);
213:         if (!item) throw Error("source_unavailable");
214:         const result: EngineeringWait = { ...rest, decision_ref, decision: item.decision };
215:         if (!terminal.has(mission.state) && ["open", "deferred"].includes(wait.state) && principal.principal_id === mission.owner_principal_id && principal.write_scopes.includes(mission.scope_id)) {
216:           const token = randomBytes(32).toString("base64url"); const expires = now + 5 * 60_000;
217:           this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM engineering_interactions WHERE expires_at<?").run(now);
218:           this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM engineering_interactions WHERE mission_id=? AND rowid IN (SELECT rowid FROM engineering_interactions WHERE mission_id=? ORDER BY issued_at DESC,rowid DESC LIMIT -1 OFFSET 31)").run(mission.mission_id, mission.mission_id);
219:           this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_interactions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(attentionDigest(token), principal.principal_id, principal.client_session_id, mission.mission_id, mission.revision, wait.wait_id, item.item_id, decision_ref.item_revision, decision_ref.content_hash, now, expires);
220:           result.interaction_token = token; result.token_expires_at = expires;
221:         }
222:         return result;
223:       });
224:       const limit = input.limit ?? 50; const rows = this.db.prepare("SELECT seq,kind,actor,payload_json,created_at FROM engineering_events WHERE mission_id=? AND seq>? ORDER BY seq LIMIT ?").all(mission.mission_id, input.cursor ?? 0, limit + 1) as Array<{ seq: number; kind: string; actor: string | null; payload_json: string; created_at: number }>;
225:       const events = rows.slice(0, limit).map(({ payload_json, ...row }) => ({ ...row, payload: JSON.parse(payload_json) as unknown }));
226:       return { mission, waits, events, cursor: events.at(-1)?.seq ?? input.cursor ?? 0, has_more: rows.length > limit,
227:         cost_evidence: { status: "unknown" as const, hard_cap_enforced: false as const, reason: "provider_usage_not_metered_by_adoption_pilot" as const },
228:         snapshot_digest: snapshot ? attentionDigest(snapshot) : null, source_version: snapshot?.version ?? null, turn_id: snapshot?.turn_id ?? null,
229:         observation_proof: mission.observation_proof_json ? JSON.parse(mission.observation_proof_json) as EngineeringObservationProof : null };
230:     });
231:   }
232:   decide(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringDecideRequest, now = Date.now()): EngineeringActionReceipt {
233:     const input = EngineeringDecideRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now); this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
234:     return this.write(() => {
235:       let mission = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
236:       const prior = this.request(principal, input.action_id, "decide", input);
237:       if (prior) return this.receipt(mission, input.action_id, true);
238:       this.steer(principal, mission); this.cas(mission, input.if_revision); this.controlBudget(mission.mission_id);
239:       const wait = this.wait(mission.mission_id, input.wait_id);
240:       if (!["open", "deferred"].includes(wait.state) || wait.action_id) throw Error("stale_decision");
241:       const ref = JSON.parse(wait.decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
242:       if (attentionDigest(ref) !== attentionDigest(input.decision_ref)) throw Error("stale_decision");
243:       const token = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_interactions WHERE token_hash=?").get(attentionDigest(input.interaction_token)) as TokenRow | undefined;
244:       if (!token || token.principal_id !== principal.principal_id || token.client_session_id !== principal.client_session_id || token.mission_id !== mission.mission_id
245:         || token.mission_revision !== input.if_revision || token.wait_id !== wait.wait_id || token.item_id !== ref.item_id || token.item_revision !== ref.item_revision || token.content_hash !== ref.content_hash) throw Error("interaction_mismatch");
246:       if (now < token.issued_at || now > token.expires_at) throw Error("interaction_expired");
247:       const { session, snapshot } = this.source(mission);
248:       if (session.version !== ref.source_version || snapshot.turn_id !== ref.turn_id || attentionDigest(SessionDecision.parse(snapshot.decision)) !== ref.content_hash) throw Error("stale_decision");
249:       const decision = this.ordinaryDecision(snapshot.decision);
250:       let text: string;
251:       if (input.answer.kind === "choice") {
252:         const choiceId = input.answer.choice_id; const choice = decision.choices.find(c => c.choice_id === choiceId);
253:         if (!choice) throw Error("invalid_choice"); text = choice.label;
254:       } else {
255:         if (decision.choices.length > 0 || (decision.continuation && decision.continuation.gate_kind !== "external_input")) throw Error("explicit_choice_required");
256:         text = input.answer.text;
257:       }
258:       const reservation = new AttentionActions(this.attention, this.owner).reserve({ scope_id: mission.scope_id, item_id: ref.item_id, action_id: input.action_id, revision: ref.item_revision, content_hash: ref.content_hash, text, takeover: false }, now);
259:       if (reservation.replay || reservation.reservation_version !== ref.source_version + 1) throw Error("action_conflict");
260:       this.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO attention_dispatch_phases VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,1,'reserved',?,1,?,?,?,?,NULL,NULL,NULL,?,?)`).run(input.action_id, reservation.message_id, mission.mission_id, principal.principal_id, principal.client_session_id,
261:         mission.source_instance_id, ref.turn_id, ref.source_version, reservation.reservation_version, this.owner.generation, now, now);
262:       this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET state='answer_recorded',action_id=?,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE wait_id=?").run(input.action_id, now, wait.wait_id);
263:       this.event(mission.mission_id, "answer_recorded", principal.principal_id, { action_id: input.action_id, message_id: reservation.message_id, wait_id: wait.wait_id, decision_ref: ref, client_session_id: principal.client_session_id, interaction_token_hash: attentionDigest(input.interaction_token), payload_digest: attentionDigest(input.answer) }, now);
264:       mission = this.bump(mission.mission_id, "answer_recorded", now);
265:       this.saveRequest(principal, input.action_id, "decide", input, mission.mission_id, { action_id: input.action_id }, now);
266:       return { mission, action_id: input.action_id, message_id: reservation.message_id, state: "reserved", replay: false, reservation_version: reservation.reservation_version };
267:     });
268:   }
269:   private receipt(mission: EngineeringMission, actionId: string, replay: boolean): EngineeringActionReceipt {
270:     const action = this.db.prepare("SELECT a.* FROM attention_actions a JOIN engineering_waits w ON w.action_id=a.action_id WHERE a.action_id=? AND w.mission_id=?").get(actionId, mission.mission_id) as ActionRow | undefined;
271:     if (!action) throw Error("not_found");
272:     const phase = this.getDispatch(action.message_id);
273:     const message = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(action.message_id);
274:     const resolved = this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?").get(action.message_id);
275:     const state = phase?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch"
276:       ? isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(this.db, action.message_id) ? "cancelled_before_dispatch" : "needs_reconciliation"
277:       : resolved ? "abandoned_unknown"
278:       : phase?.phase === "reserved" ? action.state === "reserved" && message?.state === "accepted" ? "reserved" : "needs_reconciliation"
279:       : message?.state === "applied" ? "applied" : "uncertain";
280:     return { mission, action_id: actionId, message_id: action.message_id, state, replay, reservation_version: phase?.reservation_version ?? 0 };
281:   }
282:   commitDispatch(actionId: string, principal: EngineeringPrincipal, now = Date.now()) {
283:     this.clock(now);
284:     return this.write(() => {
285:       const phase = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE action_id=?").get(actionId) as ReturnType<EngineeringStore["getDispatch"]>;
286:       if (!phase) throw Error("dispatch_gate_required");
287:       const mission = this.readable(principal, phase.mission_id); this.steer(principal, mission);
288:       if (phase.principal_id !== principal.principal_id || phase.client_session_id !== principal.client_session_id) throw Error("permission_denied");
289:       if (phase.phase !== "reserved") throw Error(phase.phase === "dispatch_committed" ? "dispatch_already_committed" : "cancelled_original_answer");
290:       if (terminal.has(mission.state) || mission.stop_intent_id) throw Error("cancelled_original_answer");
291:       if (mission.policy_digest !== POLICY_DIGEST) throw Error("policy_changed");
292:       const { session, snapshot } = this.source(mission);
293:       const action = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?").get(actionId) as ActionRow | undefined;
294:       const message = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(phase.message_id);
295:       const wait = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_waits WHERE action_id=? AND mission_id=?").get(actionId, mission.mission_id) as WaitRow | undefined;
296:       if (!action || action.state !== "reserved" || action.scope_id !== mission.scope_id || action.message_id !== phase.message_id || !message || message.state !== "accepted"
297:         || message.session_id !== mission.session_id || message.turn_id !== phase.original_turn_id || !wait || wait.state !== "answer_recorded" || wait.item_id !== action.item_id
298:         || phase.protocol_version !== 1 || phase.source_instance_id !== mission.source_instance_id || phase.source_generation !== mission.source_generation
299:         || session.version !== phase.reservation_version || snapshot.turn_id !== phase.original_turn_id || snapshot.state !== "awaiting_decision") throw Error("needs_reconciliation");
300:       if (this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?").get(phase.message_id)) throw Error("receipt_resolved");
301:       const ref = JSON.parse(wait.decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
302:       const decision = this.ordinaryDecision(snapshot.decision);
303:       if (attentionDigest(decision) !== ref.content_hash || ref.source_version !== phase.source_version) throw Error("source_changed");
304:       const payload = JSON.parse(message.payload_json) as { text?: unknown };
305:       if (typeof payload.text !== "string" || action.payload_hash !== attentionDigest({ scope_id: mission.scope_id, item_id: ref.item_id, action_id: actionId, revision: ref.item_revision, content_hash: ref.content_hash, text: payload.text, takeover: false })) throw Error("payload_changed");
306:       const changed = this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_dispatch_phases SET phase='dispatch_committed',committed_by_generation=?,updated_at=? WHERE action_id=? AND phase='reserved'").run(this.owner.generation, now, actionId);
307:       if (changed.changes !== 1) throw Error("dispatch_already_committed");
308:       this.event(mission.mission_id, "dispatch_committed", principal.principal_id, { action_id: actionId, message_id: message.message_id, daemon_generation: this.owner.generation }, now);
309:       const permit: EngineeringDispatchPermit = Object.freeze({ message_id: message.message_id, daemon_generation: this.owner.generation }); this.permits.add(permit);
310:       return { permit, session_id: mission.session_id, message_id: message.message_id, turn_id: phase.original_turn_id, reservation_version: phase.reservation_version, text: payload.text, action_id: actionId };
311:     });
312:   }
313:   consumeDispatchPermit(permit: EngineeringDispatchPermit | undefined, messageId: string): void {
314:     this.attention.assertOwner(this.owner);
315:     if (!permit || permit.message_id !== messageId || permit.daemon_generation !== this.owner.generation || !this.permits.delete(permit)) throw Error("dispatch_gate_required");
316:   }
317:   defer(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringDeferRequest, now = Date.now()) {
318:     const input = EngineeringDeferRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now); this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
319:     return this.write(() => {
320:       const mission = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
321:       if (this.request(principal, input.request_id, "defer", input)) return { mission, replay: true };
322:       this.steer(principal, mission); this.cas(mission, input.if_revision); this.controlBudget(mission.mission_id);
323:       const { snapshot } = this.source(mission);
324:       const wait = this.wait(mission.mission_id, input.wait_id); const revisit = Date.parse(input.revisit_at);
325:       if (wait.revision !== input.wait_revision || !["open", "deferred"].includes(wait.state) || wait.action_id) throw Error("stale_decision");
326:       const ref = JSON.parse(wait.decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
327:       if (snapshot.turn_id !== ref.turn_id || snapshot.version !== ref.source_version || attentionDigest(snapshot.decision) !== ref.content_hash) throw Error("stale_decision");
328:       if (revisit <= now || revisit > now + 7 * 86400_000) throw Error("invalid_revisit_at");
329:       this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET state='deferred',revision=revision+1,revisit_at=?,reason=?,reminder_sent=0,updated_at=? WHERE wait_id=?").run(revisit, input.reason, now, wait.wait_id);
330:       this.event(mission.mission_id, "decision_deferred", principal.principal_id, { wait_id: wait.wait_id, revisit_at: input.revisit_at, reason: input.reason }, now);
331:       const updated = this.bump(mission.mission_id, mission.state, now); this.saveRequest(principal, input.request_id, "defer", input, mission.mission_id, {}, now);
332:       return { mission: updated, replay: false };
333:     });
334:   }
335:   cancel(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringCancelRequest, now = Date.now()): EngineeringStopResult { return this.end(principal, EngineeringCancelRequest.parse(value), false, now); }
336:   endObservation(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringEndObservationRequest, now = Date.now()): EngineeringStopResult { return this.end(principal, EngineeringEndObservationRequest.parse(value), true, now); }
337:   private end(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, input: EngineeringCancelRequest, observationOnly: boolean, now: number): EngineeringStopResult {
338:     this.clock(now); this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
339:     return this.write(() => {
340:       const mission = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id); const operation = observationOnly ? "endObservation" : "cancel";
341:       const prior = this.request(principal, input.request_id, operation, input);
342:       if (prior) return { ...(JSON.parse(prior.response_json) as EngineeringStopResult), mission, replay: true };
343:       const isOwner = mission.owner_principal_id === principal.principal_id;
344:       if (observationOnly) {
345:         if (!isOwner) { this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "operator"); this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "stop"); }
346:       } else {
347:         this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "stop");
348:         if (!isOwner) this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "operator");
349:       }
350:       // Cancellation remains possible after another terminal observation; it does
351:       // not depend on the receipt being resolved or the Worker being active.
352:       if (mission.revision !== input.if_revision) throw Error("version_conflict");
353:       const phases = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE mission_id=?").all(mission.mission_id) as NonNullable<ReturnType<EngineeringStore["getDispatch"]>>[];
354:       const intent = randomUUID(); const kind = observationOnly ? "observation_ended" : "stop_requested";
355:       const eventSeq = this.event(mission.mission_id, kind, principal.principal_id, { stop_intent_id: intent, request_id: input.request_id, run_id: mission.run_id, session_id: mission.session_id, action_ids: phases.map(p => p.action_id), owner_generation: this.owner.generation, reason: input.reason ?? null }, now);
356:       this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_missions SET stop_intent_id=? WHERE mission_id=?").run(intent, mission.mission_id);
357:       for (const phase of phases) {
358:         if (phase.phase !== "reserved") continue;
359:         const action = this.db.prepare("SELECT state,message_id FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?").get(phase.action_id) as { state: string; message_id: string } | undefined;
360:         const message = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(phase.message_id);
361:         if (!action || action.state !== "reserved" || action.message_id !== phase.message_id || message?.state !== "accepted" || message.session_id !== mission.session_id
362:           || this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?").get(phase.message_id)) continue;
363:         const proof = { version: 1, prior_phase: "reserved", action_id: phase.action_id, message_id: phase.message_id, stop_intent_id: intent, event_seq: eventSeq, owner_generation: this.owner.generation };
364:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_dispatch_phases SET phase='cancelled_before_dispatch',stop_intent_id=?,proof_json=?,updated_at=? WHERE action_id=? AND phase='reserved'").run(intent, JSON.stringify(proof), now, phase.action_id);
365:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_actions SET state='cancelled_before_dispatch',updated_at=? WHERE action_id=?").run(now, phase.action_id);
366:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_items SET state='answer_cancelled',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id IN (SELECT item_id FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?)").run(now, phase.action_id);
367:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET state='cancelled_before_dispatch',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE action_id=?").run(now, phase.action_id);
368:       }
369:       const updated = this.bump(mission.mission_id, observationOnly ? "observation_ended" : "cancel_requested", now);
370:       const result: EngineeringStopResult = { mission: updated, replay: false, stop_intent_id: intent, run_id: mission.run_id, session_id: mission.session_id, action_ids: phases.map(p => p.action_id) };
371:       this.saveRequest(principal, input.request_id, operation, input, mission.mission_id, result, now); return result;
372:     });
373:   }
374:   recordStopReceipt(missionId: string, intentId: string, status: "stop_requested" | "stop_unknown", evidence: unknown, now = Date.now()): void {
375:     this.write(() => {
376:       const mission = this.getMission(missionId);
377:       if (mission.stop_intent_id !== intentId) throw Error("stop_intent_changed");
378:       this.event(missionId, status === "stop_unknown" ? "stop_unknown" : "stop_request_acknowledged", null, { stop_intent_id: intentId, evidence }, now);
379:     });
380:   }
381:   reconcile(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringReconcileRequest, now = Date.now()) {
382:     const input = EngineeringReconcileRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now); const initial = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
383:     if (input.kind === "query_action") return this.attention.write(this.owner, () => this.receipt(initial, input.action_id, true));
384:     return this.write(() => {
385:       const mission = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
386:       if (this.request(principal, input.request_id, "dispose_wait", input)) return { mission, replay: true };
387:       this.steer(principal, mission); this.cas(mission, input.if_revision); this.controlBudget(mission.mission_id);
388:       const wait = this.wait(mission.mission_id, input.wait_id);
389:       if (wait.revision !== input.wait_revision || wait.action_id || cleared.has(wait.state)) throw Error("receipt_requires_reconciliation");
390:       if (this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_actions WHERE item_id=?").get(wait.item_id)) throw Error("receipt_requires_reconciliation");
391:       const { snapshot } = this.source(mission);
392:       if (snapshot.version !== input.observed_source_version || snapshot.turn_id !== input.observed_turn_id || attentionDigest(snapshot) !== input.observed_snapshot_digest) throw Error("source_changed");
393:       const item = this.attention.getItem(mission.scope_id, wait.item_id); if (!item) throw Error("source_unavailable"); this.ordinaryDecision(item.decision);
394:       this.setWait(wait, "superseded", now);
395:       this.event(mission.mission_id, "steering_recorded", principal.principal_id, { wait_id: wait.wait_id, disposition: input.disposition, observed_snapshot_digest: input.observed_snapshot_digest, reason: input.reason, delivery_verified: false }, now);
396:       const updated = this.bump(mission.mission_id, "observing", now); this.saveRequest(principal, input.request_id, "dispose_wait", input, mission.mission_id, {}, now);
397:       return { mission: updated, replay: false };
398:     });
399:   }
400:   /** Must be called from the same transaction that marks the original message
401:    * applied and writes its new Session turn. This is observation, not delivery. */
402:   recordCompletion(messageId: string, completedTurnId: string, sessionVersion: number, now = Date.now()): void {
403:     if (!this.db.inTransaction) throw Error("completion_transaction_required");
404:     this.attention.assertOwner(this.owner);
405:     const phase = this.getDispatch(messageId); if (!phase) return;
406:     if (phase.phase !== "dispatch_committed") throw Error("dispatch_inconsistent");
407:     const mission = this.getMission(phase.mission_id); const { snapshot } = this.source(mission);
408:     const message = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(messageId);
409:     if (message?.state !== "applied" || snapshot.turn_id !== completedTurnId || snapshot.version !== sessionVersion || sessionVersion !== phase.reservation_version + 1) throw Error("completion_mismatch");
410:     const proof: EngineeringObservationProof = { kind: "observed_turn", action_id: phase.action_id, message_id: messageId, source_instance_id: phase.source_instance_id, source_generation: phase.source_generation, completed_turn_id: completedTurnId, session_version: sessionVersion, output_sha256: attentionDigest(snapshot.assistant_text) };
411:     this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_completions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(phase.action_id, messageId, mission.mission_id, completedTurnId, sessionVersion, JSON.stringify(proof), now);
412:     this.event(mission.mission_id, "answer_applied", null, proof, now);
413:     this.refresh(mission.mission_id, now);
414:   }
415:   /** Deterministic bounded projection; empty polls neither invoke a model nor
416:    * increment the Mission's intent revision. */
417:   refresh(missionId: string, now = Date.now()): void {
418:     this.attention.write(this.owner, () => {
419:       let mission = this.getMission(missionId); if (terminal.has(mission.state)) return;
420:       let snapshot: SessionSnapshot;
421:       try { snapshot = this.source(mission).snapshot; }
422:       catch {
423:         for (const wait of this.waits(missionId)) if (!cleared.has(wait.state)) this.setWait(wait, "needs_reconciliation", now);
424:         if (mission.state !== "needs_reconciliation") this.bump(missionId, "needs_reconciliation", now); return;
425:       }
426:       let uncertain = false;
427:       for (const wait of this.waits(missionId)) {
428:         if (cleared.has(wait.state)) continue;
429:         if (wait.state === "needs_reconciliation") uncertain = true;
430:         if (wait.state === "deferred" && wait.revisit_at !== null && now >= wait.revisit_at && now >= wait.updated_at && wait.reminder_sent === 0) {
431:           this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET reminder_sent=1 WHERE wait_id=? AND reminder_sent=0").run(wait.wait_id);
432:           this.event(missionId, "decision_reminder_due", null, { wait_id: wait.wait_id, revisit_at: wait.revisit_at, reminder_budget: 1 }, now);
433:         }
434:         if (wait.action_id) {
435:           const action = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?").get(wait.action_id) as ActionRow | undefined;
436:           if (!action) { this.setWait(wait, "needs_reconciliation", now); uncertain = true; continue; }
437:           const phase = this.getDispatch(action.message_id); const completion = this.db.prepare("SELECT proof_json FROM engineering_completions WHERE action_id=?").get(wait.action_id) as { proof_json: string } | undefined;
438:           if (completion && new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(action.message_id)?.state === "applied") { this.setWait(wait, "cleared", now); continue; }
439:           if (phase?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch" && isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(this.db, action.message_id)) { this.setWait(wait, "cancelled_before_dispatch", now); continue; }
440:           const receipt = new AttentionActions(this.attention, this.owner).reconcile(action, now);
441:           if (receipt.state === "reserved") continue;
442:           this.setWait(wait, "delivery_unknown", now); uncertain = true;
443:         } else {
444:           const ref = JSON.parse(wait.decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
445:           if (snapshot.turn_id !== ref.turn_id || snapshot.version !== ref.source_version || snapshot.decision === null || attentionDigest(snapshot.decision) !== ref.content_hash) { this.setWait(wait, "needs_reconciliation", now); uncertain = true; }
446:         }
447:       }
448:       if (snapshot.state === "awaiting_decision" && snapshot.decision) {
449:         try { this.addWait(mission, snapshot, now); } catch { uncertain = true; }
450:       }
451:       const waits = this.waits(missionId); const unclosed = waits.some(wait => !cleared.has(wait.state));
452:       const completion = this.db.prepare("SELECT proof_json FROM engineering_completions WHERE mission_id=? AND completed_turn_id=? AND session_version=?").get(missionId, snapshot.turn_id, snapshot.version) as { proof_json: string } | undefined;
453:       if (!uncertain && !unclosed && !mission.stop_intent_id && completion && snapshot.decision === null && ["idle", "completed"].includes(snapshot.state)) {
454:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_missions SET observation_proof_json=? WHERE mission_id=?").run(completion.proof_json, missionId);
455:         mission = this.bump(missionId, "completed", now); this.event(missionId, "observation_completed", null, JSON.parse(completion.proof_json), now);
456:       } else {
457:         const state = uncertain ? "needs_reconciliation" : waits.some(w => ["open", "deferred"].includes(w.state)) ? "awaiting_decision" : waits.some(w => w.state === "answer_recorded") ? "answer_recorded" : "observing";
458:         if (mission.state !== state) this.bump(missionId, state, now);
459:       }
460:       this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_missions SET observed_at=? WHERE mission_id=?").run(now, missionId);
461:     });
462:   }
463: }

79: function parseSession(row: RawSessionRow): AgentSessionRowType {
80:   return AgentSessionRow.parse({ ...row, resume_supported: row.resume_supported === 1 });
81: }
82: 
83: function canonicalJson(value: unknown): string {
84:   if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return JSON.stringify(value);
85:   if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(canonicalJson).join(",")}]`;
86:   const entries = Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
87:     .sort(([left], [right]) => left.localeCompare(right));
88:   return `{${entries.map(([key, item]) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonicalJson(item)}`).join(",")}}`;
89: }

168:     const payloadJson = canonicalJson(payload);

178:       if (existing) {

205:         ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
206:       `).run(

208:         payloadJson, row.state, row.created_at, row.applied_at,
209:       );
210:       return { outcome: "applied", session_version: expectedVersion + 1 };
211:     });

354:     const values: Array<string | number> = [];
355:     if (filter.agent_id !== undefined) {
356:       where.push("agent_id = ?");
357:       values.push(filter.agent_id);
358:     }
359:     if (filter.state !== undefined) {
360:       AgentSessionState.parse(filter.state);
361:       where.push("state = ?");
362:       values.push(filter.state);
363:     }
364:     if (filter.task_id !== undefined) {
365:       where.push("task_id = ?");
366:       values.push(filter.task_id);
367:     }
368:     const limit = filter.limit ?? 200;
369:     if (!Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 200) {
370:       throw new Error("session list limit must be an integer between 1 and 200");
371:     }
372:     values.push(limit);
373:     const sql = `SELECT * FROM agent_sessions${where.length > 0 ? ` WHERE ${where.join(" AND ")}` : ""} ORDER BY updated_at DESC, session_id LIMIT ?`;
374:     return (this.db.prepare(sql).all(...values) as RawSessionRow[]).map(parseSession);
375:   }
376: 
377:   private updateSession(
378:     sessionId: string,
379:     expectedVersion: number,
380:     updatedAt: number,
381:     assignment: string,
382:     values: unknown[],
383:   ): AgentSessionRowType {
384:     return withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
385:       const result = db.prepare(`
386:         UPDATE agent_sessions
387:         SET ${assignment}, version = version + 1, updated_at = ?
388:         WHERE session_id = ? AND version = ?
389:       `).run(...values, updatedAt, sessionId, expectedVersion);
390:       if (result.changes !== 1) {
391:         const actual = db.prepare("SELECT version FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
392:           .get(sessionId) as { version: number } | undefined;
393:         throw new SessionVersionConflict(sessionId, expectedVersion, actual?.version ?? null);
394:       }
395:       return this.readSession(db, sessionId);
396:     });
397:   }
398: 
399:   private readSession(db: Database.Database, sessionId: string): AgentSessionRowType {
400:     const row = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
401:       .get(sessionId) as RawSessionRow | undefined;
402:     if (!row) throw new SessionVersionConflict(sessionId, 0, null);
403:     return parseSession(row);
404:   }
405: }

760: async function handleSessionSend(
761:   ctx: DaemonContext,
762:   req: DaemonRequest,
763: ): Promise<SingleResponse> {
764:   const p = parseSessionSendParams(req.params);
765:   if (p instanceof Error) return singleErr(req.id, "bad_request", p.message);
766:   // Completed receipt replay remains read-only while drained; no new/retried
767:   // instruction may reserve a message or invoke an adapter during an upgrade.
768:   if (ctx.draining && ctx.sessions.getMessage(p.message_id)?.state !== "applied") {
769:     return singleErr(req.id, "draining", "daemon is not accepting session instructions");
770:   }
771: 
772:   const session = ctx.sessions.getSession(p.session_id);
773:   if (session === undefined) return singleErr(req.id, "not_found", "session not found");
774:   if (p.runtime_id !== session.agent_id) {
775:     return singleErr(req.id, "runtime_mismatch", "session belongs to another runtime");
776:   }
777:   // Idempotency wins over moving state: a retry may arrive after the first
778:   // delivery already appended a newer turn or completed the conversation.
779:   // Let the repository compare the complete canonical envelope before any
780:   // current-turn/current-state guards reject it.
781:   const existingMessage = ctx.sessions.getMessage(p.message_id);
782:   if (existingMessage !== undefined) {
783:     try {
784:       const replay = ctx.sessions.applyInbound({
785:         message_id: p.message_id,
786:         session_id: p.session_id,
787:         turn_id: p.turn_id,
788:         direction: "inbound",
789:         kind: "instruction.send",
790:         payload: { text: p.text, ...(p.requested_by === undefined ? {} : { requested_by: p.requested_by }) },
791:         state: "accepted",
792:         created_at: Date.now() / 1000,
793:       }, p.expected_version, Date.now() / 1000);
794:       if (existingMessage.state === "applied") {
795:         return singleOk(req.id, { state: replay.outcome, session_version: replay.session_version });
796:       }
797:       if (replay.session_version !== p.expected_version + 1) {
798:         return singleErr(req.id, "version_conflict", "accepted message cannot be recovered safely");
799:       }
800:       if (existingMessage.state === "accepted") {
801:         return ctx.activeSessionSends.has(session.session_id)
802:           ? singleErr(req.id, "session_busy", "session is already applying another instruction")
803:           : singleErr(
804:               req.id,
805:               "delivery_uncertain",
806:               "the previous delivery may have reached the agent; inspect the session before using a new message_id",
807:             );
808:       }
809:       if (existingMessage.state !== "failed") {
810:         return singleErr(req.id, "version_conflict", "reserved message is not retryable");
811:       }
812:       return singleErr(req.id, "delivery_uncertain", "Failed execution may have had side effects; reconcile the existing receipt before retrying");
813:     } catch (err) {
814:       if (err instanceof SessionMessageIdConflict) {
815:         return singleErr(req.id, "message_id_conflict", "message_id was already used for different content");
816:       }
817:       throw err;
818:     }
819:   }
820:   if (TERMINAL_SESSION_STATES.has(session.state)) {
821:     return singleErr(req.id, "session_terminal", `session state=${session.state}`);
822:   }
823:   if (!session.resume_supported || session.native_session_id === null) {
824:     return singleErr(req.id, "resume_unsupported", "session cannot be resumed");
825:   }
826:   const snapshot = ctx.sessions.latestSnapshot(p.session_id);
827:   if (snapshot.turn_id !== p.turn_id) {
828:     return singleErr(req.id, "turn_conflict", "message does not target the current turn");
829:   }
830:   const adapter = ctx.adapters.get(session.agent_id);
831:   if (adapter === undefined || !isSessionCapableAdapter(adapter) || !adapter.sessionCapabilities().resume) {
832:     return singleErr(req.id, "resume_unsupported", "agent does not support resume");
833:   }
834:   if (ctx.activeSessionSends.has(session.session_id)) {
835:     return singleErr(req.id, "session_busy", "session is already applying another instruction");
836:   }
837: 
838:   // Refuse busy/denied admission before minting an instruction receipt.
839:   let admission: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof acquireManualSessionAdmission>>;
840:   try { admission = await acquireManualSessionAdmission(ctx, session, p.message_id); }
841:   catch { return singleErr(req.id, "admission_denied", "Session execution admission was denied; no instruction was reserved"); }
842:   let handedOff = false;
843:   try {
844:     if (ctx.draining || admission.controller.signal.aborted) return singleErr(req.id, "admission_denied", "Session execution was cancelled; no instruction was reserved");
845:     let applied: { outcome: "applied" | "duplicate"; session_version: number };
846:     const now = Date.now() / 1000;
847:     try {
848:       applied = reserveSessionInstruction(ctx.db, ctx.dbPath, {
849:         message_id: p.message_id,
850:         session_id: p.session_id,
851:         turn_id: p.turn_id,
852:         direction: "inbound",
853:         kind: "instruction.send",
854:         payload: { text: p.text, ...(p.requested_by === undefined ? {} : { requested_by: p.requested_by }) },
855:         state: "accepted",
856:         created_at: now,
857:       }, p.expected_version, now);
858:     } catch (err) {
859:       if (err instanceof SessionVersionConflict) {
860:         return singleErr(req.id, "version_conflict", "session version changed; refresh before retrying");
861:       }
862:       if (err instanceof SessionMessageIdConflict) {
863:         return singleErr(req.id, "message_id_conflict", "message_id was already used for different content");
864:       }
865:       if (err instanceof Error && ["delivery_uncertain", "continuation_in_flight", "grant_owned", "signed_approval_required", "invalid_decision"].includes(err.message)) return singleErr(req.id, err.message, "Refresh the decision and use its authorized owner route");
866:       throw err;
867:     }
868:     if (applied.outcome === "duplicate") {
869:       return ctx.sessions.getMessage(p.message_id)?.state === "applied"
870:         ? singleOk(req.id, { state: "duplicate", session_version: applied.session_version })
871:         : singleErr(req.id, "delivery_uncertain", "A receipt appeared during admission; inspect it before retrying");
872:     }
873: 
874:     handedOff = true;
875:     return await executeReservedSessionTurn(ctx, req, p, session, adapter, applied.session_version, { admittedLease: admission.lease, controller: admission.controller });
876:   } finally { if (!handedOff) await admission.release(); }

880: /** Track queued manual work before awaiting admission so drain/cancel can stop it.
881:  * No durable receipt exists until admission succeeds; callers transfer both
882:  * ownership and the same cancellation signal to the reserved-turn executor.
883:  */
884: async function acquireManualSessionAdmission(
885:   ctx: DaemonContext,
886:   session: NonNullable<ReturnType<SessionRepository["getSession"]>>,
887:   pendingMessageId: string,
888: ) {
889:   const controller = new AbortController();
890:   ctx.activeSessionSends.set(session.session_id, pendingMessageId);
891:   ctx.activeRuns.set(session.run_id, controller);
892:   const clear = () => {
893:     if (ctx.activeSessionSends.get(session.session_id) === pendingMessageId) ctx.activeSessionSends.delete(session.session_id);
894:     if (ctx.activeRuns.get(session.run_id) === controller) ctx.activeRuns.delete(session.run_id);
895:   };
896:   try {
897:     const lease = await acquireSessionAdmission(ctx, session.agent_id, session.repo_path, session.run_id, controller.signal);
898:     return { lease, controller, release: async () => { try { await lease.release(); } finally { clear(); } } };
899:   } catch (error) {
900:     clear();
901:     throw error;
902:   }
903: }

905: /** Private port: only an already persisted reservation may invoke the runtime. */
906: async function executeReservedSessionTurn(
907:   ctx: DaemonContext,
908:   req: DaemonRequest,
909:   p: SessionSendParams,
910:   session: NonNullable<ReturnType<SessionRepository["getSession"]>>,
911:   adapter: SessionCapableAdapter,
912:   reservationVersion: number,
913:   options: {
914:     admittedLease?: ExecutionLease;
915:     controller?: AbortController;
916:     completed?: (turnId: string, version: number, now: number) => void;
917:     engineeringPermit?: Parameters<import("@osstack/core").EngineeringStore["consumeDispatchPermit"]>[0];
918:   } = {},
919: ): Promise<SingleResponse> {
920:   const applied = { session_version: reservationVersion };
921:   ctx.activeSessionSends.set(session.session_id,p.message_id);
922:   const controller = options.controller ?? new AbortController();
923:   const now = Date.now() / 1000;
924:   ctx.activeRuns.set(session.run_id, controller);
925:   let lease: ExecutionLease | undefined = options.admittedLease;
926:   let started = false;
927:   let settled = false;
928:   try {
929:     // This guard stays active even when the feature flag is off. An extended
930:     // receipt can enter this helper only with the one-use CAS winner's permit.
931:     const engineeringPhase = ctx.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE message_id=?").get(p.message_id);
932:     if (engineeringPhase) {
933:       if (!ctx.engineering || !options.engineeringPermit) return singleErr(req.id, "dispatch_gate_required", "Use the original engineering action; this receipt cannot be sent through another route");
934:       try { ctx.engineering.store.consumeDispatchPermit(options.engineeringPermit, p.message_id); }
935:       catch { return singleErr(req.id, "dispatch_gate_required", "The dispatch permit is invalid or already consumed"); }
936:     }
937:     lease = options.admittedLease ?? await acquireSessionAdmission(ctx, adapter.id, session.repo_path, session.run_id, controller.signal);
938:     const failReservedMessage = (
939:       code: string,
940:       message: string,
941:     ): SingleResponse => {
942:       try {
943:         ctx.sessions.markMessageFailed(p.message_id);
944:       } catch {
945:         return singleErr(
946:           req.id,
947:           "delivery_uncertain",
948:           "the agent failed and the reservation could not be finalized; inspect the session before retrying",
949:         );
950:       }
951:       return singleErr(req.id, code, message);
952:     };
953:     if (session.native_session_id === null) return failReservedMessage("resume_unsupported", "session cannot resume");
954:     const context: Record<string, unknown> = {};
955:     if (session.repo_path !== "") context["repo_path"] = session.repo_path;
956:     if (p.requested_by !== undefined) context["requested_by"] = p.requested_by;
957:     const input: AgentSessionInput = {
958:       session_id: session.session_id,
959:       task_id: session.task_id,
960:       run_id: session.run_id,
961:       turn_id: p.turn_id,
962:       native_session_id: session.native_session_id,
963:       expected_version: applied.session_version,
964:       message_id: p.message_id,
965:       prompt: p.text,
966:       ...(Object.keys(context).length === 0 ? {} : { context }),
967:     };
968:     const publicEvents: AgentSessionEvent[] = [];
969:     let assistantText = "";
970:     let decision: unknown = null;
971:     let terminalCompleted = false;
972:     try {
973:       started = true;
974:       for await (const rawEvent of boundedAdapterEvents(adapter.resumeSession(input, controller.signal), controller.signal)) {
975:         const event = AgentSessionEventSchema.parse(rawEvent);
976:         if (event.type === "native_session.started") {
977:           if (event.native_session_id !== session.native_session_id) {
978:             return failReservedMessage(
979:               "adapter_protocol",
980:               "agent resumed an unexpected native session",
981:             );
982:           }
983:           continue;
984:         }
985:         if (JSON.stringify(event).includes(session.native_session_id)) {
986:           return failReservedMessage(
987:             "adapter_protocol",
988:             "agent output contained protected session metadata",
989:           );
990:         }
991:         if (event.type === "session.terminal") {
992:           if (event.state !== "completed") {
993:             const rawCode = event.error_code ?? "unknown";
994:             const safeCode = /^[a-z0-9_]{1,64}$/.test(rawCode) ? rawCode : "unknown";
995:             return failReservedMessage(
996:               "agent_failed",
997:               `agent failed while applying the instruction (${safeCode})`,
998:             );
999:           }
1000:           terminalCompleted = true;
1001:         }
1002:         if (event.type === "assistant.message") {
1003:           // Controlled results are opaque data; their adapter emits decisions separately.
1004:           const directive = isControlledSessionAdapter(adapter) ? undefined : parseDecisionDirective(event.text);
1005:           if (directive !== undefined) {
1006:             publicEvents.push(directive);
1007:             decision = {
1008:               decision_id: directive.decision_id,
1009:               question: directive.question,
1010:               choices: directive.choices,
1011:               ...(directive.continuation === undefined ? {} : { continuation: directive.continuation }),
1012:             };
1013:             assistantText = directive.question;
1014:             continue;
1015:           }
1016:           assistantText += event.text;
1017:         }
1018:         publicEvents.push(event);
1019:         if (event.type === "decision.requested") decision = {
1020:           decision_id: event.decision_id,
1021:           question: event.question,
1022:           choices: event.choices,
1023:           ...(event.continuation === undefined ? {} : { continuation: event.continuation }),
1024:         };
1025:       }
1026:       settled = true;
1027:       if (!terminalCompleted) {
1028:         return failReservedMessage(
1029:           "agent_failed",
1030:           "agent ended without completing the instruction",
1031:         );
1032:       }
1033:     } catch {
1034:       return failReservedMessage(
1035:         "agent_failed",
1036:         "agent failed while applying the instruction",
1037:       );
1038:     }
1039: 
1040:     const finalState = publicEvents.some((event) => event.type === "decision.requested")
1041:       ? "awaiting_decision"
1042:       : "idle";
1043:     const completedAt = Date.now() / 1000;
1044:     const turnId = `turn_${randomUUID()}`;
1045:     const updatedSession = ctx.db.transaction(() => {
1046:       const updated = ctx.sessions.completeInboundTurn({
1047:         turn: {
1048:           turn_id: turnId,
1049:           session_id: session.session_id,
1050:           ordinal: (ctx.db.prepare(
1051:             "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(ordinal), 0) + 1 AS ordinal FROM session_turns WHERE session_id = ?",
1052:           ).get(session.session_id) as { ordinal: number }).ordinal,
1053:           state: finalState,
1054:           assistant_text: assistantText,
1055:           decision,
1056:           created_at: now,
1057:           updated_at: completedAt,
1058:         },
1059:         message_id: p.message_id,
1060:         expected_version: applied.session_version,
1061:         applied_at: completedAt,
1062:       });
1063:       options.completed?.(turnId, updated.version, completedAt);
1064:       if (engineeringPhase) ctx.engineering!.store.recordCompletion(p.message_id, turnId, updated.version);
1065:       return updated;
1066:     })();
1067:     return singleOk(req.id, {
1068:       state: "applied",
1069:       session_version: updatedSession.version,
1070:       events: publicEvents,
1071:     });
1072:   } catch {
1073:     try { ctx.sessions.markMessageFailed(p.message_id); }
1074:     catch {
1075:       return singleErr(req.id, "delivery_uncertain", "Execution receipt could not be finalized; inspect persisted state before retrying");
1076:     }
1077:     return singleErr(req.id, started ? "delivery_uncertain" : "admission_denied", "Session execution could not be confirmed; inspect persisted state before retrying");
1078:   } finally {
1079:     try {
1080:       if (started && (!settled || controller.signal.aborted)) {
1081:         controller.abort();
1082:         if (await confirmAdapterStopped(adapter, session.run_id)) await lease?.release();
1083:         else lease?.retain("session resume exit unconfirmed");
1084:       } else await lease?.release();
1085:     } finally {
1086:       ctx.activeSessionSends.delete(session.session_id);
1087:       if (ctx.activeRuns.get(session.run_id) === controller) ctx.activeRuns.delete(session.run_id);
1088:       requestSupervisionScan(ctx, "session.send.settled");
1089:     }
1090:   }
1091: }
1092: 
1093: function requestSupervisionScan(ctx: DaemonContext, reason: string): void {
1094:   if (ctx.draining || !ctx.supervision) return;
1095:   void ctx.supervision.requestScan(reason).catch(() => {
1096:     // The periodic pass is the recovery path; never fail a persisted session reply.
1097:   });
1098: }

1104: async function getAttentionTriage(ctx: DaemonContext) {
1105:   if (!ctx.supervision || ctx.draining) throw new Error("attention_unavailable");
1106:   ctx.attentionTriage ??= (async () => {
1107:     const {createAttentionTriageService} = await import("./attention-triage.js");
1108:     const {AttentionTriageLedger} = await import("@osstack/core");
1109:     if (!ctx.supervision || ctx.draining) throw new Error("attention_unavailable");
1110:     const enabled = process.env["OSSTACK_ATTENTION_TRIAGE_ENABLED"] === "1";
1111:     const model = enabled ? process.env["OSSTACK_ATTENTION_TRIAGE_MODEL"] ?? "" : "disabled";
1112:     const maxCost = enabled ? Number(process.env["OSSTACK_ATTENTION_TRIAGE_MAX_COST_USD"] ?? 0.05) : 0.05;
1113:     if (!Number.isFinite(maxCost) || maxCost <= 0 || maxCost > 0.25) throw new Error("invalid_triage_configuration");
1114:     try {
1115:     const service = createAttentionTriageService({
1116:       ledger:new AttentionTriageLedger(ctx.supervision.store,ctx.supervision.observer.owner),
1117:       config:{enabled,model_id:model,calls_per_hour:enabled ? Number(process.env["OSSTACK_ATTENTION_TRIAGE_CALLS_PER_HOUR"] ?? 5) : 5,
1118:         timeout_ms:enabled ? Number(process.env["OSSTACK_ATTENTION_TRIAGE_TIMEOUT_MS"] ?? 20000) : 20000},
1119:       providerFactory:ctx.attentionTriageProviderFactory ?? (async () => {
1120:         const {createControlledTriageProvider} = await import("@osstack/agents");
1121:         const {createScratchDir} = await import("@osstack/core");
1122:         return createControlledTriageProvider({state_directory:await createScratchDir("runtime","attention-triage"),model_id:model,max_cost_usd:maxCost});
1123:       }),
1124:     });
1125:     return service;
1126:     } catch { throw new Error("invalid_triage_configuration"); }
1127:   })();
1128:   return ctx.attentionTriage;
1129: }

1131: /** Only for reads whose final DB access is fenced by an aborted signal. */
1132: async function drainCancelledReads(reads: Promise<unknown>[]): Promise<void> {
1133:   let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
1134:   try {
1135:     await Promise.race([
1136:       Promise.allSettled(reads),
1137:       new Promise<void>(resolve => { timer = setTimeout(resolve, 2000); }),
1138:     ]);
1139:   } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
1140: }
